# Agents · R · part 29

978 entries, review-performance to REVIEW.template. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **review-performance**: Reviews clear performance defects — N+1 queries, quadratic patterns on large collections, blocking I/O on hot paths, unbounded caches, frame-budget overruns and per-frame allocation churn in real-time render loops. Activate for API/server/full-stack projects, and for projects with a real-time rendering/game-loop path. ([neolao/claude-plugin-vibe](https://github.com/neolao/claude-plugin-vibe/blob/HEAD/agents/review-performance.md))
- **review-performance**: Reviews code for performance issues — unnecessary copies, allocation in hot paths, algorithmic complexity, cache efficiency ([nmohamaya/companion_software_stack](https://github.com/nmohamaya/companion_software_stack/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-performance.md))
- **review-performance**: Reviews a git diff for performance problems - queries in loops, blocking async, unbounded data, repeated work. Read-only. ([olivierpicault/ostack](https://github.com/olivierpicault/ostack/blob/HEAD/agents/review-performance.md))
- **review-performance**: Reviews omorfos for performance, read-only, over a scope it is given, and answers with findings in the form .claude/skills/review/reviewer.md sets. Started by the review skill, one of several at once. ([omorfos/desktop](https://github.com/omorfos/desktop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-performance.md))
- **review-performance**: パフォーマンスを専門的に評価するエージェント。ボトルネック、メモリ管理、キャッシング、レンダリング効率を検証します。 ([sendo-kakeru/omojan](https://github.com/sendo-kakeru/omojan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-performance.md))
- **review-performance**: Performance reviewer. Identifies bottlenecks, inefficient algorithms, unnecessary I/O, memory issues, and scalability ceilings. ([smi-th-sms/main](https://github.com/smi-th-sms/main/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-performance.md))

([zaakirio/my-ai-config](https://github.com/zaakirio/my-ai-config/blob/HEAD/agents/review-performance.md))
- **review-performance-checker**: Performance review agent that checks planned algorithmic complexity, obvious regressions, data structure choices, and long-function/long-logic decomposition limits. - **review-performance-scalability**: Use this agent when `/review-before-pr` needs performance review, when the user requests `--performance`, or when changes touch async flows, queries, rendering hotspots, AI pipeline latency, or loading-cost risks. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: A PR changes data fetching and rendering-heavy code. user: "/review-before-pr" assistant: "I'll use the review-performance-scalability agent to inspect waterfalls, cache opportunities, and avoidable latency." &lt;commentary&gt; Full review mode should include a performance pass to catch regressions and obvious latency/cost issues early. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example… ([VoMinhKhoii/Kallo](https://github.com/VoMinhKhoii/Kallo/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/review-performance-scalability.md) · ★3)
- **review-persona**: Read-only, plugin-distributed content-reasoning subagent for a fixed, enumerated set of call sites -- see this file's own "Sanctioned call sites" section below for the exact, current list. - **review-perspective**: Applies one review perspective, supplied by a review workflow skill, to a code diff, direct file or directory selection, or implementation plan. - **review-perspective-finder**: Use this agent to discover what review perspectives (観点) a code change should be reviewed against. Reads the diff and surrounding code, then returns a structured list of perspectives — each with a title, why it matters, and where to focus. Read-only; does NOT review the code itself or modify files. Invoked by the multi-agent-review skill. Input should specify the diff range / changed files and any task description. ([rnnno/dotfiles](https://github.com/rnnno/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/review-perspective-finder.md))

- **review-phone**: Reviews an Orbital change for the phone — that the feature has a phone decision, that a phone ahead of its Mac, a Mac ahead of its phone and the relay between them still work together, that the allowlist matches what the phone calls, and whether the change ships over the air or needs a native build. Used by the orbital-code-review skill. ([slothworks-apps/orbital](https://github.com/slothworks-apps/orbital/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-phone.md) · ★4)

Dispatch with exactly `review CHANGES in &lt;file&gt;` (git-diff hunks only, after writing or editing) or `review FULL &lt;file&gt;` (the entire file); no other context needed. - **review-plan**: Ты ревьюишь implementation plans и JSON task records, составленные другим агентом. Будь критичен. ([mrvladd-d/memobank](https://github.com/mrvladd-d/memobank/blob/HEAD/skills/_shared/agents/review-plan.md) · ★56)
- **review-plan**: Review implementation plans for structural integrity before coding begins. Catches: unverified assumptions, missing phases, wrong approach, ambiguous instructions, incomplete code path coverage, missing verification strategy. Run BEFORE implementation to catch plan-level errors that cost hours to fix later. ([spinje/pflow](https://github.com/spinje/pflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-plan.md) · ★17)
- **review-plan**: Use after a plan has been written to verify it fully covers the requirements and design. Spawns parallel sub-agent reviewers for security, requirements coverage, code smells, and pattern consistency — acts as a gate before handing a plan off to implementation agents. ([crouton-labs/sisyphus](https://github.com/crouton-labs/sisyphus/blob/HEAD/templates/agent-plugin/agents/review-plan.md) · ★4)
- **review-plan**: Reviews implementation plans for completeness, correctness, functional gaps, standards, regression risk, robustness, architectural gaps, and TDD quality. Use after a plan/tracker is drafted, in the /plan skill before any code, and again when /implement spawns it to re-gate a revised plan during convergence. ([KashZod/devloop](https://github.com/KashZod/devloop/blob/HEAD/agents/review-plan.md) · ★3)
- **review-plan**: Reviews implementation plans for completeness, correctness, regression risk, robustness, blindspots (security, privacy, platform checks), maintainability, and TDD quality. Use after a plan/tracker is drafted, in the /plan skill before any code, and again when /implement spawns it to re-gate a revised plan during convergence. ([OneKashLabs/devloop](https://github.com/OneKashLabs/devloop/blob/HEAD/agents/review-plan.md) · ★3)
- **review-plan**: PLAN.md と plan.json のレビュー。構成・一貫性・詳細度・パス検証を行い、問題リストを返す。 ([ryryo/dot-claude-dev](https://github.com/ryryo/dot-claude-dev/blob/HEAD/.claude/.backup/skills/dev/epic/agents/review-plan.md) · ★3)
- **review-plan**: Reviews a planning document for completeness, feasibility, and architectural soundness. Acts as a quality gate — returns PASS or REJECTED with specific issues. ([AbsolutSystems/absolutpowers](https://github.com/AbsolutSystems/absolutpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/review-plan.md) · ★1)
- **review-plan**: You plan the review of one large file's diff before it is reviewed. You do not file findings. You read the diff and the checklist and emit a short, severity-ordered list of where a reviewer should look hardest. Ported from open-code-review's plan step (D3, shape parity): a unit whose diff is large enough gets this pass so the review pass that follows reads with focus. ([cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools](https://github.com/cjbischoff/cjbischoff-claude-code-tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/sec-overlay/skills/sec-overlay/agents/review-plan.md))
- **review-plan**: Review an existing plan file against the real codebase and report its findings, each classified as mechanical or decision. Spawns review-plan-facts for the claims the code settles, then audits architecture and coverage itself. Writes nothing; the session that spawned it records the outcome. Spawn it with the plan file path; plan-task runs it automatically as its last step. ([ds-usova/tdd-sdlc](https://github.com/ds-usova/tdd-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/review-plan.md))
- **review-plan**: Reviews a drafted plan (product and implementation) for gaps, scope creep, and fit with this project's stack before it's presented to the user for sign-off. Use during phase 2 of /deliver, before showing a plan for approval, or whenever asked to sanity-check a plan. ([Eduardo-Barreto/web-project-template](https://github.com/Eduardo-Barreto/web-project-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-plan.md))
- **review-plan**: Adversarial reviewer for implementation plans. Finds blind spots, architectural risks, untested assumptions, and feasibility issues. Builds an independent plan blind before comparing (TWO-STEPS), or mentally analyzes before critiquing (ONE-STEP). ([pbdotfiles/chezmoi](https://github.com/pbdotfiles/chezmoi/blob/HEAD/cm/dot_config/opencode/agents/review-plan.md))
- **review-plan**: Pre-implementation plan reviewer. Catches plan defects before delegation to implementer agents — wrong file hints, broken integration contracts, missing scope, weak reference patterns, unflagged risks. Use when a planned task touches more than 3 files, involves IR invariants or type system changes, or when the orchestrator is uncertain about file hint accuracy. Reads project docs (AGENTS.md, glossary, design docs, decisions) at review time to apply the project's terminology and design priorities. Read-only tools only. Do NOT use to review completed code — use review-code for that. ([pcone/pi-config](https://github.com/pcone/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agents/review-plan.md))
- **review-plan**: Validate a task plan against the actual codebase before implementation ([Robert-Freire/langTeachSaaS](https://github.com/Robert-Freire/langTeachSaaS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-plan.md))
- **review-plan**: Reviews a feature plan page with no chat history, against the plan checklist, the KB and the code, and lists the gaps and the questions a builder would hit. Use when the user says "review the plan". ([roland-b19/life-tracker](https://github.com/roland-b19/life-tracker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/review-plan.md))
- **review-plan-ablation-judge**: Logical-equivalence judge for the review-plan ablation experiment. Compares two plan reviews — one from the structured question-based control, one from the directive-based ablated variant — on 5 criteria that measure whether the ablation silently drops, adds, or misweights issues relative to the control. Spawned by ablate-review-plan skill for each fixture. Receives CONTROL_REVIEW, ABLATED_REVIEW, and EXPECTED_FINDING (may be empty for real-world fixtures). Returns JSON only — no prose. NOT for standalone use. ([whichguy/claude-craft](https://github.com/whichguy/claude-craft/blob/HEAD/plugins/review-bench/agents/review-plan-ablation-judge.md) · ★2)

- **review-verifier**: Verifier lane of the qol-code-review engine. Spawned with a prompt that names one job file; tries to refute one review claim with cited code and replies with one JSON verdict. - **review-verifier**: Verifies one finding from the expert-review panel against the actual code and returns confirmed, rejected or (security only) provisional with a reason. - **review-verifier**: Adversarially checks review findings on a MoonShine pull request - tries to refute each one from the code, tests and specs, and returns a verdict per finding.
